ump-helper (the program beside Ultimate MP3 Player Installer that signs and installs) - the Rust libraries
inside it, each under its own license. Where a library offers a choice of licenses, it's used under the first
one it names. The Apache License 2.0 text is at the start, once, for every library under it; every other notice
is each library's own file.
